================================================================================
            BFP / LNS SYSTOLIC ARRAY  -  DETAILED TEST REPORT
================================================================================
  Datapath : 4x4 output-stationary, log-domain (LNS) multiply
  Encoding : v = (-1)^s * 2^E * 2^L   (E shared per block, L per element)
  Metric   : condition-aware error = |got-exp| / sum|terms|
  Tolerance: 10%
================================================================================
